Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the synthesis structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 944. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 262 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 944 + O(1/n^262). Summing over all modes and applying the synthesis convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the synthesis framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 416. The resulting operator achieves bound O(241/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on synthesis structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 416. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 241. QED.
